</p> <a class="font-black lg:mr-4 no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/the-best-sneakerhead-packaging">Read More</a></div> <p class="border-b-2 border-black font-black leading-tiny mb-px uppercase"><a class="no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/the-best-sneakerhead-packaging">Shawn Binder</a> <span class="hidden md:inline">— 10/21/2019</span></p></div></div></div><div aria-hidden="true" class="tns-item" data-index="7" data-type="post" tabindex="-1"><div class="dieline mb-0"><div class="dieline-full lg:dieline-left"><a class="no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/highball-brings-sophistication-to-booze-free-cocktails"><div class="dogear"><img alt="" class="block h-auto w-full" src="https://ucarecdn.com/a70f5c60-3333-444d-8ed9-ef43d71d746b/-/crop/1499x843/0,79/-/preview//"/></div></a></div> <div class="dieline-center lg:dieline-right flex flex-col justify-between"><div class="mb-8"><p class="border-b-2 border-black font-black leading-tiny pt-leading-fix text-lg uppercase"><a class="category-title-link" href="https://thedieline.com/blog?c=Beverage">Beverage</a>, <a class="category-title-link" href="https://thedieline.com/blog?c=Spirits+%26+Liquor">Spirits &amp; Liquor</a></p> <a class="no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/highball-brings-sophistication-to-booze-free-cocktails"><h2 class="font-light my-8 lg:mr-4 text-4xl">
Highball Brings Sophistication To Booze-Free Cocktails
</h2></a> <p class="lg:mr-4 mb-4 leading-normal">
With the help of design and brand agency Path, The Original Free Drinks Company developed Highball, a ready-to-drink, alcohol-free line that stays true to the cocktail experience with authentic flavors.
</p> <a class="font-black lg:mr-4 no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/highball-brings-sophistication-to-booze-free-cocktails">Read More</a></div> <p class="border-b-2 border-black font-black leading-tiny mb-px uppercase"><a class="no-underline text-black" href="/blog/2019/10/21/highball-brings-sophistication-to-booze-free-cocktails">Rudy Sanchez</a> <span class="hidden md:inline">— 10/21/2019</span></p></div></div></div></div></div></div> <div class="hidden slider-custom-controls md:flex md:items-center md:justify-between md:text-3xl" data-v-ac056cb2=""><a class="custom-controls-prev cursor-pointer no-underline text-black" data-v-ac056cb2=""><img alt="Left" class="block h-auto w-6" data-v-ac056cb2="" src="/images/chevron-right.svg"/></a> <a class="custom-controls-next cursor-pointer no-underline text-black" data-v-ac056cb2=""><img alt="Right" class="block h-auto w-6" data-v-ac056cb2="" src="/images/chevron-right.svg"/></a></div></div></section>

<section class="my-24">
<h2 class="font-black mb-6 text-3xl md:text-6xl">
Best of the Week
</h2>
<div class="grid-full gap-4/2 overflow-hidden md:grid-1/4">
<article>
<a href="https://thedieline.com/blog/2019/10/17/google-teams-up-with-dominos-to-create-high-tech-pizza-topping">
